namespace HandlerGui
{
    partial class Form1
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Required designer variable.
        /// </summary>
        private System.ComponentModel.IContainer components = null;

        /// <summary>
        /// Clean up any resources being used.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="disposing">true if managed resources should be disposed; otherwise, false.</param>
        protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
        {
            if (disposing && (components != null))
            {
                components.Dispose();
            }
            base.Dispose(disposing);
        }

        #region Windows Form Designer generated code

        /// <summary>
        /// Required method for Designer support - do not modify
        /// the contents of this method with the code editor.
        /// </summary>
        private void InitializeComponent()
        {
            this.components = new System.ComponentModel.Container();
            this.AutoScaleMode = System.Windows.Forms.AutoScaleMode.Font;
            this.Text = "Form1";
        }

        #endregion
    }

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 14:23:15)*

The UI in those screenshots is the classic **ClickOnce deployment UI** from .NET Framework 2.0–4.x. It follows the Windows XP/Vista visual style:

- fixed-size dialog (no resize)
- white content area
- light gray background (`SystemColors.Control`)
- bold Segoe UI/Tahoma 8.25 pt headings
- thin etched separators
- icon on the right
- standard Windows buttons
- no modern flat controls
- classic progress bar

Instead of a generic `Form1`, I'd create **three forms**:

```
HandlerGui
│
├── InstallConfirmationForm.cs
├── DownloadProgressForm.cs
├── LaunchingForm.cs
└── Resources
    shield.png
    globe.png
    computer.png
```

## Confirmation Form

Approximately **500×320**.

```
 --------------------------------------------------------
| Application Install - Security Warning             [X] |
|--------------------------------------------------------|
|                                                        |
| Publisher cannot be verified.                          |
| Are you sure you want to install this application?     |
|                                            (globe)     |
|                                                        |
| Name:        MyApplication                             |
| From:        https://example.com                       |
| Publisher:   Unknown Publisher                         |
|--------------------------------------------------------|
| (shield) While applications can be useful...           |
|          More Information...                           |
|                                                        |
|                             [ Install ] [Don't Install]|
 --------------------------------------------------------
```

Controls:

```csharp
Label lblTitle;
Label lblQuestion;

Label lblNameCaption;
Label lblFromCaption;
Label lblPublisherCaption;

Label lblName;
Label lblFrom;
Label lblPublisher;

PictureBox pictureRight;
PictureBox pictureShield;

LinkLabel linkMoreInformation;

Button btnInstall;
Button btnCancel;
```

---

## Installing Form

Approx **500×240**

```
 ---------------------------------------------------------
| (82%) Installing WindowsApplication1                [X] |
|---------------------------------------------------------|
|                                                         |
| Installing WindowsApplication1                          |
| This may take several minutes...                        |
|                                              (globe)    |
|                                                         |
| Name:   WindowsApplication1                             |
| From:   vbts7                                           |
|                                                         |
| ████████████████████████████                            |
|                                                         |
| Downloading: 11.7 MB of 22.4 MB                         |
|                                                         |
|                                         [ Cancel ]      |
 ---------------------------------------------------------
```

Controls

```csharp
Label lblHeader;
Label lblDescription;

PictureBox pictureStatus;

Label lblNameCaption;
Label lblName;

Label lblFromCaption;
Label lblFrom;

ProgressBar progressBar;

Label lblDownload;

Button btnCancel;
```

---

## Launching Form

Approx **400×150**

```
 -------------------------------------------
| Launching Application                 [X] |
|-------------------------------------------|
|                                           |
|   PC ----------- Globe                    |
|                                           |
| Verifying application requirements...     |
| This may take a few moments.              |
|                                           |
 -------------------------------------------
```

Controls

```csharp
PictureBox pictureComputer;
PictureBox pictureGlobe;

ProgressBar progressLine;

Label lblStatus;
```

Use a ProgressBar with:

```csharp
Style = ProgressBarStyle.Marquee;
MarqueeAnimationSpeed = 30;
Height = 6;
```

or draw a thin green line manually if you want it to look closer to ClickOnce.

---

## Base styling

All forms should share:

```csharp
FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
MaximizeBox = false;
MinimizeBox = false;
ShowInTaskbar = false;
StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
BackColor = SystemColors.Control;
Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25F);
```

Content panel:

```csharp
Panel panelContent = new Panel();

panelContent.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
panelContent.BackColor = Color.White;
```

Separator:

```csharp
Label separator = new Label();

separator.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.Fixed3D;
separator.Height = 2;
separator.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
```

Buttons:

```csharp
btnInstall.Size = new Size(88,26);
btnCancel.Size = new Size(88,26);

btnInstall.UseVisualStyleBackColor = true;
btnCancel.UseVisualStyleBackColor = true;
```

---

## Title fonts

```csharp
new Font("Tahoma", 8.25f, FontStyle.Bold)
```

Description:

```csharp
new Font("Tahoma", 8.25f, FontStyle.Regular)
```

---

## Progress bar

Use the standard Windows progress bar:

```csharp
progressBar.Style = ProgressBarStyle.Continuous;
progressBar.Minimum = 0;
progressBar.Maximum = 100;
progressBar.Value = 82;
```

---

This structure will produce a UI that is very close to the original ClickOnce installer dialogs from Windows XP/Vista. I would also recommend creating a common `InstallerFormBase` class that provides the shared layout (white content panel, separators, fonts, dialog settings, and bottom button area), with the three forms inheriting from it and only supplying their specific controls.

Here is a complete `Form1` turned into the **Confirmation Form**. It is self-contained and should compile as a normal WinForms form.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace HandlerGui
{
    public partial class Form1 : Form
    {
        public Form1()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            // Example values — replace with your own data
            lblNameValue.Text = "WindowsApplication1";
            lblFromValue.Text = "vbts7";
            lblPublisherValue.Text = "Unknown Publisher";

            linkMoreInformation.LinkClicked += linkMoreInformation_LinkClicked;
        }

        private void btnInstall_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
            Close();
        }

        private void btnDontInstall_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel;
            Close();
        }

        private void linkMoreInformation_LinkClicked(object sender, LinkLabelLinkClickedEventArgs e)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                "More information would normally open here.",
                "More Information",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        }
    }

    partial class Form1
    {
        private System.ComponentModel.IContainer components = null;

        private Panel panelMain;
        private Panel panelBottom;
        private Panel panelHeader;
        private Panel panelDetails;

        private Label lblTitle;
        private Label lblQuestion;

        private Label lblNameCaption;
        private Label lblFromCaption;
        private Label lblPublisherCaption;

        private Label lblNameValue;
        private Label lblFromValue;
        private Label lblPublisherValue;

        private PictureBox pictureRight;
        private PictureBox pictureShield;

        private LinkLabel linkMoreInformation;

        private Button btnInstall;
        private Button btnDontInstall;

        private Label separatorTop;
        private Label separatorBottom;

        protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
        {
            if (disposing && (components != null))
            {
                components.Dispose();
            }
            base.Dispose(disposing);
        }

        private void InitializeComponent()
        {
            this.components = new System.ComponentModel.Container();
            this.panelMain = new Panel();
            this.panelHeader = new Panel();
            this.panelDetails = new Panel();
            this.panelBottom = new Panel();
            this.lblTitle = new Label();
            this.lblQuestion = new Label();
            this.lblNameCaption = new Label();
            this.lblFromCaption = new Label();
            this.lblPublisherCaption = new Label();
            this.lblNameValue = new Label();
            this.lblFromValue = new Label();
            this.lblPublisherValue = new Label();
            this.pictureRight = new PictureBox();
            this.pictureShield = new PictureBox();
            this.linkMoreInformation = new LinkLabel();
            this.btnInstall = new Button();
            this.btnDontInstall = new Button();
            this.separatorTop = new Label();
            this.separatorBottom = new Label();

            ((System.ComponentModel.ISupportInitialize)(this.pictureRight)).BeginInit();
            ((System.ComponentModel.ISupportInitialize)(this.pictureShield)).BeginInit();
            this.SuspendLayout();

            // 
            // Form1
            // 
            this.AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.Font;
            this.BackColor = SystemColors.Control;
            this.ClientSize = new Size(508, 322);
            this.FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
            this.MaximizeBox = false;
            this.MinimizeBox = false;
            this.Name = "Form1";
            this.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
            this.Text = "Application Install - Security Warning";
            this.Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25F, FontStyle.Regular, GraphicsUnit.Point);
            this.ShowInTaskbar = true;

            // 
            // panelMain
            // 
            this.panelMain.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            this.panelMain.BackColor = SystemColors.Control;
            this.panelMain.Padding = new Padding(0);
            this.panelMain.Controls.Add(this.panelDetails);
            this.panelMain.Controls.Add(this.panelHeader);
            this.panelMain.Controls.Add(this.separatorBottom);
            this.panelMain.Controls.Add(this.panelBottom);
            this.panelMain.Controls.Add(this.separatorTop);

            // 
            // separatorTop
            // 
            this.separatorTop.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
            this.separatorTop.Height = 2;
            this.separatorTop.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.Fixed3D;
            this.separatorTop.Margin = new Padding(0);

            // 
            // panelHeader
            // 
            this.panelHeader.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
            this.panelHeader.BackColor = Color.White;
            this.panelHeader.Height = 110;
            this.panelHeader.Padding = new Padding(18, 18, 18, 0);
            this.panelHeader.Controls.Add(this.pictureRight);
            this.panelHeader.Controls.Add(this.lblTitle);
            this.panelHeader.Controls.Add(this.lblQuestion);

            // 
            // lblTitle
            // 
            this.lblTitle.AutoSize = true;
            this.lblTitle.Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25F, FontStyle.Bold, GraphicsUnit.Point);
            this.lblTitle.Location = new Point(18, 18);
            this.lblTitle.Name = "lblTitle";
            this.lblTitle.Size = new Size(226, 13);
            this.lblTitle.Text = "Publisher cannot be verified.";

            // 
            // lblQuestion
            // 
            this.lblQuestion.AutoSize = true;
            this.lblQuestion.Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25F, FontStyle.Regular, GraphicsUnit.Point);
            this.lblQuestion.Location = new Point(18, 40);
            this.lblQuestion.Name = "lblQuestion";
            this.lblQuestion.Size = new Size(287, 13);
            this.lblQuestion.Text = "Are you sure you want to install this application?";

            // 
            // pictureRight
            // 
            this.pictureRight.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Top | AnchorStyles.Right;
            this.pictureRight.Location = new Point(424, 10);
            this.pictureRight.Name = "pictureRight";
            this.pictureRight.Size = new Size(48, 48);
            this.pictureRight.SizeMode = PictureBoxSizeMode.CenterImage;
            this.pictureRight.Image = SystemIcons.Information.ToBitmap();

            // 
            // panelDetails
            // 
            this.panelDetails.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
            this.panelDetails.BackColor = Color.White;
            this.panelDetails.Height = 118;
            this.panelDetails.Padding = new Padding(18, 10, 18, 0);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.pictureShield);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.lblNameCaption);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.lblFromCaption);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.lblPublisherCaption);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.lblNameValue);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.lblFromValue);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.lblPublisherValue);
            this.panelDetails.Controls.Add(this.linkMoreInformation);

            // 
            // pictureShield
            // 
            this.pictureShield.Location = new Point(18, 48);
            this.pictureShield.Name = "pictureShield";
            this.pictureShield.Size = new Size(16, 16);
            this.pictureShield.SizeMode = PictureBoxSizeMode.CenterImage;
            this.pictureShield.Image = SystemIcons.Warning.ToBitmap();

            // 
            // lblNameCaption
            // 
            this.lblNameCaption.AutoSize = true;
            this.lblNameCaption.Location = new Point(42, 10);
            this.lblNameCaption.Name = "lblNameCaption";
            this.lblNameCaption.Size = new Size(36, 13);
            this.lblNameCaption.Text = "Name:";

            // 
            // lblFromCaption
            // 
            this.lblFromCaption.AutoSize = true;
            this.lblFromCaption.Location = new Point(42, 32);
            this.lblFromCaption.Name = "lblFromCaption";
```

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 14:40:24)*

Below are two WinForms classes matching the same design language as the confirmation dialog: a classic ClickOnce-style **Installing** dialog and a **Launching** dialog. Both are self-contained and use only standard WinForms controls.

---

# InstallingForm.cs

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace HandlerGui
{
    public class InstallingForm : Form
    {
        Label lblHeader;
        Label lblDescription;

        Label lblNameCaption;
        Label lblName;

        Label lblFromCaption;
        Label lblFrom;

        Label lblDownload;

        ProgressBar progressBar;

        Button btnCancel;

        PictureBox pictureStatus;

        public InstallingForm()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void InitializeComponent()
        {
            Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25f);

            ClientSize = new Size(500, 210);
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
            MaximizeBox = false;
            MinimizeBox = false;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
            Text = "(0%) Installing Application";
            BackColor = SystemColors.Control;

            var whitePanel = new Panel();
            whitePanel.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
            whitePanel.Height = 165;
            whitePanel.BackColor = Color.White;
            Controls.Add(whitePanel);

            pictureStatus = new PictureBox();
            pictureStatus.Location = new Point(430, 12);
            pictureStatus.Size = new Size(48, 48);
            pictureStatus.SizeMode = PictureBoxSizeMode.CenterImage;
            pictureStatus.Image = SystemIcons.Information.ToBitmap();
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(pictureStatus);

            lblHeader = new Label();
            lblHeader.AutoSize = true;
            lblHeader.Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25f, FontStyle.Bold);
            lblHeader.Location = new Point(15, 15);
            lblHeader.Text = "Installing WindowsApplication1";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblHeader);

            lblDescription = new Label();
            lblDescription.Location = new Point(15, 35);
            lblDescription.Size = new Size(360, 32);
            lblDescription.Text = "This may take several minutes. You can use your computer to do other tasks during the installation.";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblDescription);

            lblNameCaption = new Label();
            lblNameCaption.Location = new Point(35, 78);
            lblNameCaption.AutoSize = true;
            lblNameCaption.Text = "Name:";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblNameCaption);

            lblName = new Label();
            lblName.Location = new Point(110, 78);
            lblName.AutoSize = true;
            lblName.Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25f, FontStyle.Bold);
            lblName.Text = "WindowsApplication1";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblName);

            lblFromCaption = new Label();
            lblFromCaption.Location = new Point(35, 98);
            lblFromCaption.AutoSize = true;
            lblFromCaption.Text = "From:";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblFromCaption);

            lblFrom = new Label();
            lblFrom.Location = new Point(110, 98);
            lblFrom.AutoSize = true;
            lblFrom.Text = "vbts7";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblFrom);

            progressBar = new ProgressBar();
            progressBar.Location = new Point(15, 125);
            progressBar.Size = new Size(470, 18);
            progressBar.Style = ProgressBarStyle.Continuous;
            progressBar.Minimum = 0;
            progressBar.Maximum = 100;
            progressBar.Value = 82;
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(progressBar);

            lblDownload = new Label();
            lblDownload.Location = new Point(15, 147);
            lblDownload.AutoSize = true;
            lblDownload.Text = "Downloading: 11.7 MB of 22.4 MB";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblDownload);

            var sep = new Label();
            sep.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
            sep.Height = 2;
            sep.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.Fixed3D;
            Controls.Add(sep);
            sep.BringToFront();

            btnCancel = new Button();
            btnCancel.Text = "Cancel";
            btnCancel.Size = new Size(75, 24);
            btnCancel.Location = new Point(410, 173);
            btnCancel.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Bottom | AnchorStyles.Right;
            btnCancel.Click += (_, __) => Close();
            Controls.Add(btnCancel);
        }

        public void UpdateProgress(int percent, string text)
        {
            percent = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(100, percent));

            progressBar.Value = percent;
            lblDownload.Text = text;
            Text = $"({percent}%) Installing Application";
        }
    }
}
```

Example:

```csharp
var f = new InstallingForm();

f.UpdateProgress(
    67,
    "Downloading: 15.2 MB of 22.4 MB");

f.Show();
```

# LaunchingForm.cs

```csharp
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace HandlerGui
{
    public class LaunchingForm : Form
    {
        PictureBox pictureComputer;
        PictureBox pictureGlobe;

        ProgressBar progressLine;

        Label lblStatus;

        public LaunchingForm()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }

        private void InitializeComponent()
        {
            Font = new Font("Tahoma", 8.25f);

            ClientSize = new Size(404, 150);

            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
            MaximizeBox = false;
            MinimizeBox = false;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
            BackColor = SystemColors.Control;
            Text = "Launching Application";

            var whitePanel = new Panel();
            whitePanel.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            whitePanel.BackColor = Color.White;
            Controls.Add(whitePanel);

            pictureComputer = new PictureBox();
            pictureComputer.Location = new Point(28, 28);
            pictureComputer.Size = new Size(32, 32);
            pictureComputer.Image = SystemIcons.Application.ToBitmap();
            pictureComputer.SizeMode = PictureBoxSizeMode.CenterImage;
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(pictureComputer);

            pictureGlobe = new PictureBox();
            pictureGlobe.Location = new Point(338, 28);
            pictureGlobe.Size = new Size(32, 32);
            pictureGlobe.Image = SystemIcons.Information.ToBitmap();
            pictureGlobe.SizeMode = PictureBoxSizeMode.CenterImage;
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(pictureGlobe);

            progressLine = new ProgressBar();
            progressLine.Location = new Point(62, 40);
            progressLine.Size = new Size(270, 6);
            progressLine.Style = ProgressBarStyle.Marquee;
            progressLine.MarqueeAnimationSpeed = 25;
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(progressLine);

            lblStatus = new Label();
            lblStatus.Location = new Point(28, 78);
            lblStatus.Size = new Size(340, 32);
            lblStatus.Text =
                "Verifying application requirements. This may take a few moments.";
            whitePanel.Controls.Add(lblStatus);
        }

        public void SetStatus(string text)
        {
            lblStatus.Text = text;
        }
    }
}
```

---

## Suggested improvements for an even closer ClickOnce look

The screenshots use several details that standard WinForms controls don't replicate exactly. To get much closer to the original dialogs, I'd recommend:

- Use the actual **ClickOnce globe** and **yellow shield** icons instead of `SystemIcons`.
- Replace the launching dialog's `ProgressBar` with a custom 2–3 pixel green line, since the original isn't a real progress bar.
- Use **Visual Styles** (`Application.EnableVisualStyles()`) to get the XP/Vista button appearance.
- Set `ShowInTaskbar = false` for all dialogs so they behave like installer dialogs.
- Add `AcceptButton` and `CancelButton` to mirror the original keyboard behavior.
- Use the original dialog dimensions (approximately 497×225 for Installing and 404×148 for Launching) for pixel-accurate spacing.

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 15:22:45)*

The original ClickOnce dialog **doesn't actually use a WinForms `ProgressBar`** there. It's a custom animation:

- two static icons (computer and globe)
- a thin gray line between them
- a short green segment (~20–35 px) that slides from left to right
- when it reaches the end, it wraps back to the beginning
- no fade, no acceleration—just constant speed

The closest recreation is a custom control.

```csharp
public class AnimatedTransferLine : Control
{
    private readonly Timer timer = new Timer();

    private int offset = -30;

    public Color BackgroundLineColor { get; set; } = Color.Silver;
    public Color SegmentColor { get; set; } = Color.FromArgb(120, 205, 85);

    public int SegmentWidth { get; set; } = 28;

    public int Speed { get; set; } = 2;

    public AnimatedTransferLine()
    {
        DoubleBuffered = true;
        Height = 8;

        timer.Interval = 16;       // ~60 FPS
        timer.Tick += Timer_Tick;
        timer.Start();
    }

    private void Timer_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        offset += Speed;

        if (offset > Width)
            offset = -SegmentWidth;

        Invalidate();
    }

    protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnPaint(e);

        Graphics g = e.Graphics;

        int y = Height / 2;

        using (Pen gray = new Pen(BackgroundLineColor))
        {
            g.DrawLine(gray, 0, y, Width, y);
        }

        using (Pen green = new Pen(SegmentColor, 2))
        {
            g.DrawLine(
                green,
                offset,
                y,
                offset + SegmentWidth,
                y);
        }
    }
}
```

Then simply replace the progress bar:

```csharp
AnimatedTransferLine transferLine = new AnimatedTransferLine();

transferLine.Location = new Point(70, 40);
transferLine.Size = new Size(250, 8);

whitePanel.Controls.Add(transferLine);
```

---

### To make it look **almost pixel-identical** to the ClickOnce animation:

Instead of a solid green line, use a **gradient**.

```csharp
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;

using (LinearGradientBrush brush =
       new LinearGradientBrush(
           new Rectangle(offset, y - 1, SegmentWidth, 3),
           Color.FromArgb(235,255,235),
           Color.FromArgb(60,175,55),
           LinearGradientMode.Horizontal))
{
    using (Pen p = new Pen(brush, 2))
    {
        g.DrawLine(
            p,
            offset,
            y,
            offset + SegmentWidth,
            y);
    }
}
```

That produces the light-to-dark green streak that closely matches the original.

---

### An even more faithful approach

If you zoom in on the original ClickOnce dialog, the moving part isn't a plain bar at all—it's a small **capsule-shaped "packet"** with rounded ends traveling along a thin gray line. You can recreate that by drawing a rounded rectangle (or ellipse-ended capsule) with a subtle highlight and shadow instead of a simple line segment. Combined with the gray track and the computer/globe icons, it becomes extremely difficult to distinguish from the original ClickOnce animation.

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 17:46:14)*

VS2005 is the problem here: it does **not** understand `BeforeTargets` / `AfterTargets`.  
Use the old MSBuild 2.0 pattern instead: extend `AfterBuildDependsOn` and `CleanDependsOn`.

Here is a VS2005-safe version of your **C# project** that copies the Rust DLL out of `rust-src\target\...` into the app output folder:

```xml
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <PropertyGroup>
    <Configuration Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == '' ">Debug</Configuration>
    <Platform Condition=" '$(Platform)' == '' ">AnyCPU</Platform>

    <ProductVersion>8.0.50727</ProductVersion>
    <SchemaVersion>2.0</SchemaVersion>
    <ProjectGuid>{F2E0FCDD-A2A0-4EC4-A293-DB96E44C154A}</ProjectGuid>
    <OutputType>WinExe</OutputType>
    <AppDesignerFolder>Properties</AppDesignerFolder>
    <RootNamespace>HandlerGui</RootNamespace>
    <AssemblyName>HandlerGui</AssemblyName>
    <TargetFrameworkVersion>v2.0</TargetFrameworkVersion>
    <ApplicationIcon>File.ico</ApplicationIcon>

    <!-- Rust Build Configuration -->
    <RustProjectRoot>$(ProjectDir)..\..\..\..\rust-src</RustProjectRoot>
    <RustDllName>rust9x_windows_auth.dll</RustDllName>
    <RustTargetTriple>i686-rust9x-windows-msvc</RustTargetTriple>

    <!-- Rust DLL Source Paths -->
    <RustDllDebugPath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug\$(RustDllName)</RustDllDebugPath>
    <RustDllReleasePath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release\$(RustDllName)</RustDllReleasePath>

    <!-- Runtime directories -->
    <RustRuntimeDebugDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug</RustRuntimeDebugDir>
    <RustRuntimeReleaseDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release</RustRuntimeReleaseDir>

    <!-- VS2005-compatible target chaining -->
    <AfterBuildDependsOn>$(AfterBuildDependsOn);BuildRustProject;CopyRustDll;CopyRustRuntimeFiles</AfterBuildDependsOn>
    <CleanDependsOn>$(CleanDependsOn);CleanRustFiles</CleanDependsOn>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Debug|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugSymbols>true</DebugSymbols>
    <DebugType>full</DebugType>
    <Optimize>false</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Debug\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>DEBUG;TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <AllowUnsafeBlocks>true</AllowUnsafeBlocks>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllDebugPath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeDebugDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Release|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugType>pdbonly</DebugType>
    <Optimize>true</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Release\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllReleasePath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeReleaseDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Reference Include="System" />
    <Reference Include="System.Configuration" />
    <Reference Include="System.Data" />
    <Reference Include="System.Deployment" />
    <Reference Include="System.Drawing" />
    <Reference Include="System.Windows.Forms" />
    <Reference Include="System.Xml" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <ProjectReference Include="..\Rust9xWindowsAuth\Rust9xWindowsAuth.csproj">
      <Project>{86E365E2-D855-4579-B53D-9B0DF7FF0857}</Project>
      <Name>Rust9xWindowsAuth</Name>
    </ProjectReference>
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Compile Include="ConfirmForm.cs">
      <SubType>Form</SubType>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Include="ConfirmForm.Designer.cs">
      <DependentUpon>ConfirmForm.cs</DependentUpon>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Include="InstallingForm.cs">
      <SubType>Form</SubType>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Include="InstallingForm.Designer.cs">
      <DependentUpon>InstallingForm.cs</DependentUpon>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Include="AnimatedTransferLine.cs">
      <SubType>Component</SubType>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Include="LaunchingForm.cs">
      <SubType>Form</SubType>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Include="LaunchingForm.Designer.cs">
      <DependentUpon>LaunchingForm.cs</DependentUpon>
    </Compile>
    <Compile Include="Program.cs" />
    <Compile Include="Properties\AssemblyInfo.cs" />

    <EmbeddedResource Include="ConfirmForm.resx">
      <SubType>Designer</SubType>
      <DependentUpon>ConfirmForm.cs</DependentUpon>
    </EmbeddedResource>
    <EmbeddedResource Include="InstallingForm.resx">
      <SubType>Designer</SubType>
      <DependentUpon>InstallingForm.cs</DependentUpon>
    </EmbeddedResource>
    <EmbeddedResource Include="LaunchingForm.resx">
      <SubType>Designer</SubType>
      <DependentUpon>LaunchingForm.cs</DependentUpon>
    </EmbeddedResource>

    <EmbeddedResource Include="Properties\Resources.resx">
      <Generator>ResXFileCodeGenerator</Generator>
      <LastGenOutput>Resources.Designer.cs</LastGenOutput>
      <SubType>Designer</SubType>
    </EmbeddedResource>
    <Compile Include="Properties\Resources.Designer.cs">
      <AutoGen>True</AutoGen>
      <DependentUpon>Resources.resx</DependentUpon>
      <DesignTime>True</DesignTime>
    </Compile>

    <None Include="Properties\Settings.settings">
      <Generator>SettingsSingleFileGenerator</Generator>
      <LastGenOutput>Settings.Designer.cs</LastGenOutput>
    </None>
    <Compile Include="Properties\Settings.Designer.cs">
      <AutoGen>True</AutoGen>
      <DependentUpon>Settings.settings</DependentUpon>
      <DesignTimeSharedInput>True</DesignTimeSharedInput>
    </Compile>
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Content Include="File.ico" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <!-- Copy lists split so VS2005 doesn't need transforms -->
  <ItemGroup Condition="Exists('$(RustRuntimeDir)')">
    <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.dll" />
    <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.manifest" />
    <RustRuntimeCrtFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <Import Project="$(MSBuildBinPath)\Microsoft.CSharp.targets" />

  <!-- Optional: build Rust first -->
  <Target Name="BuildRustProject" Condition="'$(BuildRust)' == 'true'">
    <Message Text="Building Rust project in $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-release" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Release' " />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-debug" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Debug' " />
  </Target>

  <!-- Copy Rust DLL from rust-src\target to C# output -->
  <Target Name="CopyRustDll">
    <Message Text="Copying Rust DLL from: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="$(RustDllSourcePath)"
          DestinationFolder="$(OutputPath)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="true"
          Condition="Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')" />

    <Error Text="Rust DLL not found at: $(RustDllSourcePath)"
           Condition="!Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')" />
  </Target>

  <!-- Copy VC80 CRT/runtime files -->
  <Target Name="CopyRustRuntimeFiles" Condition="Exists('$(RustRuntimeDir)')">
    <Message Text="Copying Rust runtime files from: $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(OutputPath)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)' != ''" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)' != ''" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="CleanRustFiles">
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)$(RustDllName)" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)$(RustDllName)')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)msvcr80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)msvcr80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)msvcp80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)msvcp80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)msvcm80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)msvcm80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <RemoveDir Directories="$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
  </Target>
</Project>
```

The important fixes are:

1. `AfterTargets` / `BeforeTargets` removed.
2. `AfterBuildDependsOn` is used instead.
3. `MSBuildProjectDirectory` replaced with `ProjectDir` for VS2005 friendliness.
4. Runtime copying is split into two plain `Copy` steps, because VS2005/MSBuild 2.0 is much happier that way.

Your Rust DLL source is still taken from:

```xml
$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release\$(RustDllName)
```

So the C# build copies the DLL **out of**:

`E:\code\rust9x-windows2000auth\rust-src\target\...`

and into the WinForms output folder.

For the Rust-side `.csproj` wrapper, use the same rule: no `AfterTargets`, no `BeforeTargets`, only old-style dependency properties.

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 17:48:23)*

Here is the VS2005-safe version of that project file. It removes `BeforeTargets` / `AfterTargets`, uses old MSBuild dependency properties, and keeps the Rust DLL/runtime copy step pointed at `rust-src\target\...`.

```xml
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <PropertyGroup>
    <Configuration Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == '' ">Debug</Configuration>
    <Platform Condition=" '$(Platform)' == '' ">AnyCPU</Platform>

    <ProductVersion>8.0.50727</ProductVersion>
    <SchemaVersion>2.0</SchemaVersion>
    <ProjectGuid>{86E365E2-D855-4579-B53D-9B0DF7FF0857}</ProjectGuid>

    <OutputType>Library</OutputType>
    <AppDesignerFolder>Properties</AppDesignerFolder>
    <RootNamespace>Rust9xWindowsAuth</RootNamespace>
    <AssemblyName>Rust9xWindowsAuth</AssemblyName>
    <TargetFrameworkVersion>v2.0</TargetFrameworkVersion>

    <!-- Rust Build Configuration -->
    <RustProjectRoot>$(ProjectDir)..\..\..\..\rust-src</RustProjectRoot>
    <RustDllName>rust9x_windows_auth.dll</RustDllName>
    <RustTargetTriple>i686-rust9x-windows-msvc</RustTargetTriple>

    <!-- Rust DLL Source Paths -->
    <RustDllDebugPath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug\$(RustDllName)</RustDllDebugPath>
    <RustDllReleasePath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release\$(RustDllName)</RustDllReleasePath>

    <!-- Rust runtime directories -->
    <RustRuntimeDebugDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug</RustRuntimeDebugDir>
    <RustRuntimeReleaseDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release</RustRuntimeReleaseDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Debug|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugSymbols>true</DebugSymbols>
    <DebugType>full</DebugType>
    <Optimize>false</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Debug\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>DEBUG;TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <AllowUnsafeBlocks>true</AllowUnsafeBlocks>
    <PlatformTarget>AnyCPU</PlatformTarget>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllDebugPath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeDebugDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Release|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugType>pdbonly</DebugType>
    <Optimize>true</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Release\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllReleasePath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeReleaseDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Reference Include="System" />
    <Reference Include="System.Configuration" />
    <Reference Include="System.Data" />
    <Reference Include="System.Xml" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Compile Include="AuthConfig.cs" />
    <Compile Include="AuthManager.cs" />
    <Compile Include="CentralFile.cs" />
    <Compile Include="Properties\AssemblyInfo.cs" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <None Include="App.config" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <Import Project="$(MSBuildBinPath)\Microsoft.CSharp.targets" />

  <!-- Old-style MSBuild extension points for VS2005 -->
  <PropertyGroup>
    <BuildDependsOn>BuildRustProject;$(BuildDependsOn)</BuildDependsOn>
    <AfterBuildDependsOn>$(AfterBuildDependsOn);CopyRustDll;CopyRustRuntimeFiles</AfterBuildDependsOn>
    <CleanDependsOn>$(CleanDependsOn);CleanRustFiles</CleanDependsOn>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <!-- Optional: build Rust first -->
  <Target Name="BuildRustProject" Condition="'$(BuildRust)' == 'true'">
    <Message Text="Building Rust project in $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-release"
          WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)"
          Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Release' " />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-debug"
          WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)"
          Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Debug' " />
  </Target>

  <!-- Copy the built Rust DLL into this project's output folder -->
  <Target Name="CopyRustDll">
    <Message Text="Copying Rust DLL from: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="To: $(OutputPath)" Importance="high" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="$(RustDllSourcePath)"
          DestinationFolder="$(OutputPath)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="true"
          Condition="Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')">
      <Output TaskParameter="DestinationFiles" ItemName="CopiedDlls" />
    </Copy>

    <Message Text="Rust DLL copied successfully." Importance="high"
             Condition="Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')" />

    <Error Text="Rust DLL not found at: $(RustDllSourcePath). Please build the Rust project first using: cargo +rust9x dll-release"
           Condition="!Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')" />
  </Target>

  <!-- Copy runtime dependencies from Rust output -->
  <ItemGroup Condition="Exists('$(RustRuntimeDir)')">
    <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.dll" Exclude="$(RustDllSourcePath);$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.pdb" />
    <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.manifest" />
    <RustRuntimeCrtFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <Target Name="CopyRustRuntimeFiles" Condition="Exists('$(RustRuntimeDir)')">
    <Message Text="Copying Rust runtime files from: $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(OutputPath)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)' != ''" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)' != ''" />

    <Message Text="Rust runtime files copied successfully." Importance="high" />
  </Target>

  <!-- Clean up copied Rust files -->
  <Target Name="CleanRustFiles">
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)$(RustDllName)"
            Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)$(RustDllName)')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)msvcr80.dll"
            Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)msvcr80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)msvcp80.dll"
            Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)msvcp80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)msvcm80.dll"
            Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)msvcm80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*"
            Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <RemoveDir Directories="$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT"
               Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <Message Text="Cleaned Rust DLL and runtime files." Importance="high" />
  </Target>
</Project>
```

The main fixes are:
- removed `AfterTargets` / `BeforeTargets`
- moved the build/copy chaining into `BuildDependsOn`, `AfterBuildDependsOn`, and `CleanDependsOn`
- kept the DLL source under `rust-src\target\...`
- split runtime copying into VS2005-friendly item groups

One thing I would still change next is the `cargo +rust9x dll-debug` / `dll-release` invocation so it does not depend on shell `cd`; the current form is already fine for VS2005.

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 17:56:13)*

Yes — this needs to be made more explicit and noisy, because old VS2005/MSBuild is easy to “succeed” while doing nothing.

The safest pattern is:

1. log the resolved paths,
2. verify the Rust DLL exists,
3. copy with a real command step,
4. and, for debugging, dump the contents of the Rust target folder.

I would change the Rust library project to use `BeforeBuild`, `AfterBuild`, and `Clean` directly, and add a small C# helper tool for copying and logging.

## 1) Fixed project file for the Rust library

This version is much more reliable in VS2005-style MSBuild:

```xml
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <PropertyGroup>
    <Configuration Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == '' ">Debug</Configuration>
    <Platform Condition=" '$(Platform)' == '' ">AnyCPU</Platform>
    <ProductVersion>8.0.50727</ProductVersion>
    <SchemaVersion>2.0</SchemaVersion>
    <ProjectGuid>{86E365E2-D855-4579-B53D-9B0DF7FF0857}</ProjectGuid>
    <OutputType>Library</OutputType>
    <AppDesignerFolder>Properties</AppDesignerFolder>
    <RootNamespace>Rust9xWindowsAuth</RootNamespace>
    <AssemblyName>Rust9xWindowsAuth</AssemblyName>
    <TargetFrameworkVersion>v2.0</TargetFrameworkVersion>

    <RustProjectRoot>$(ProjectDir)..\..\..\..\rust-src</RustProjectRoot>
    <RustDllName>rust9x_windows_auth.dll</RustDllName>
    <RustTargetTriple>i686-rust9x-windows-msvc</RustTargetTriple>

    <RustDllDebugPath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug\$(RustDllName)</RustDllDebugPath>
    <RustDllReleasePath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release\$(RustDllName)</RustDllReleasePath>

    <RustRuntimeDebugDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug</RustRuntimeDebugDir>
    <RustRuntimeReleaseDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release</RustRuntimeReleaseDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Debug|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugSymbols>true</DebugSymbols>
    <DebugType>full</DebugType>
    <Optimize>false</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Debug\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>DEBUG;TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <AllowUnsafeBlocks>true</AllowUnsafeBlocks>
    <PlatformTarget>AnyCPU</PlatformTarget>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllDebugPath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeDebugDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Release|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugType>pdbonly</DebugType>
    <Optimize>true</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Release\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllReleasePath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeReleaseDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Reference Include="System" />
    <Reference Include="System.Configuration" />
    <Reference Include="System.Data" />
    <Reference Include="System.Xml" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Compile Include="AuthConfig.cs" />
    <Compile Include="AuthManager.cs" />
    <Compile Include="CentralFile.cs" />
    <Compile Include="Properties\AssemblyInfo.cs" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <None Include="App.config" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <Import Project="$(MSBuildBinPath)\Microsoft.CSharp.targets" />

  <!-- Make the build noisy and deterministic -->
  <Target Name="BeforeBuild">
    <Message Text="=== Rust9xWindowsAuth BeforeBuild ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Configuration: $(Configuration)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Platform: $(Platform)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="ProjectDir: $(ProjectDir)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustProjectRoot: $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustDllSourcePath: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustRuntimeDir: $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />

    <Exec Command="cmd /c if exist &quot;$(RustRuntimeDir)&quot; dir /s /b &quot;$(RustRuntimeDir)&quot; > &quot;$(ProjectDir)rust-build-inputs.txt&quot; else echo RustRuntimeDir missing > &quot;$(ProjectDir)rust-build-inputs.txt&quot;"
          ContinueOnError="true" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="BuildRustProject" Condition="'$(BuildRust)' == 'true'">
    <Message Text="Building Rust project in $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-debug" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Debug' " />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-release" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Release' " />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="AfterBuild">
    <CallTarget Targets="CopyRustDll" />
    <CallTarget Targets="CopyRustRuntimeFiles" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="CopyRustDll">
    <Message Text="=== CopyRustDll ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Source: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Destination: $(TargetDir)" Importance="high" />

    <Error Text="Rust DLL not found: $(RustDllSourcePath)"
           Condition="!Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="$(RustDllSourcePath)"
          DestinationFolder="$(TargetDir)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false"
          OverwriteReadOnlyFiles="true" />

    <Exec Command="cmd /c dir /b &quot;$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)&quot;"
          ContinueOnError="true" />

    <Message Text="Rust DLL copy completed." Importance="high" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="CopyRustRuntimeFiles">
    <Message Text="=== CopyRustRuntimeFiles ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Runtime dir: $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />

    <ItemGroup Condition="Exists('$(RustRuntimeDir)')">
      <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.dll" Exclude="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.pdb" />
      <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.manifest" />
      <RustRuntimeCrtFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" />
    </ItemGroup>

    <Message Text="Root runtime files count: @(RustRuntimeRootFiles->Count())" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="CRT runtime files count: @(RustRuntimeCrtFiles->Count())" Importance="high" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(TargetDir)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false"
          OverwriteReadOnlyFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)' != ''" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false"
          OverwriteReadOnlyFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)' != ''" />

    <Exec Command="cmd /c dir /s /b &quot;$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT&quot;"
          ContinueOnError="true" />

    <Message Text="Rust runtime copy completed." Importance="high" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="Clean">
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)$(AssemblyName).dll" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)$(AssemblyName).dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcr80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcr80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcp80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcp80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcm80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcm80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <RemoveDir Directories="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <Message Text="Rust artifacts cleaned." Importance="high" />
  </Target>
</Project>
```

### What this fixes
- It logs the exact source and destination paths.
- It fails loudly if the DLL is missing.
- It uses `$(TargetDir)` rather than `$(OutputPath)` for output copy.
- It avoids the old “looks like it ran but copied nothing” problem.
- It dumps a directory listing so you can see what is actually present during the build.

---

## 2) Small C# copy tool, which is often more reliable than MSBuild copy tasks

Old MSBuild sometimes behaves badly with item expansions and directory copies. A tiny helper EXE is usually the most dependable approach.

Create a console app, for example `BuildCopyTool.cs`:

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;

namespace BuildCopyTool
{
    internal static class Program
    {
        static int Main(string[] args)
        {
            try
            {
                if (args.Length < 3)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine("Usage:");
                    Console.WriteLine("  BuildCopyTool.exe <sourceFile> <sourceDir> <destDir>");
                    return 2;
                }

                string sourceFile = args[0];
                string sourceDir = args[1];
                string destDir = args[2];

                Console.WriteLine("=== BuildCopyTool ===");
                Console.WriteLine("Source file: " + sourceFile);
                Console.WriteLine("Source dir:  " + sourceDir);
                Console.WriteLine("Dest dir:    " + destDir);

                if (!Directory.Exists(destDir))
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(destDir);
                    Console.WriteLine("Created destination directory.");
                }

                if (!File.Exists(sourceFile))
                {
                    Console.WriteLine("ERROR: DLL not found: " + sourceFile);
                    return 3;
                }

                string dllName = Path.GetFileName(sourceFile);
                string dllDest = Path.Combine(destDir, dllName);

                File.Copy(sourceFile, dllDest, true);
                Console.WriteLine("Copied DLL -> " + dllDest);

                if (Directory.Exists(sourceDir))
                {
                    CopyDirectoryFiles(sourceDir, destDir);
                }
                else
                {
                    Console.WriteLine("Runtime source dir missing: " + sourceDir);
                }

                Console.WriteLine("Done.");
                return 0;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("FATAL: " + ex);
                return 1;
            }
        }

        private static void CopyDirectoryFiles(string sourceDir, string destDir)
        {
            foreach (string file in Directory.GetFiles(sourceDir))
            {
                string name = Path.GetFileName(file);
                string dest = Path.Combine(destDir, name);
                File.Copy(file, dest, true);
                Console.WriteLine("Copied file: " + file + " -> " + dest);
            }

            string crtDir = Path.Combine(sourceDir, "Microsoft.VC80.CRT");
            if (Directory.Exists(crtDir))
            {
                string crtDest = Path.Combine(destDir, "Microsoft.VC80.CRT");
                if (!Directory.Exists(crtDest))
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(crtDest);

                foreach (string file in Directory.GetFiles(crtDir))
                {
                    string dest = Path.Combine(crtDest, Path.GetFileName(file));
                    File.Copy(file, dest, true);
                    Console.WriteLine("Copied CRT: " + file + " -> " + dest);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Then call it from MSBuild:

```xml
<Target Name="AfterBuild">
  <Exec Command="&quot;$(SolutionDir)BuildCopyTool\bin\Release\BuildCopyTool.exe&quot; &quot;$(RustDllSourcePath)&quot; &quot;$(RustRuntimeDir)&quot; &quot;$(TargetDir)&quot;" />
</Target>
```

That gives you real console output during the build, and it removes a lot of MSBuild weirdness.

---

## 3) Best practical setup

The most stable setup here is:

- Rust project builds into `rust-src\target\...`
- C# project calls a small copy tool after build
- copy tool logs everything and copies the DLL + runtime files
- C# project fails the build if the DLL is missing

That is much easier to debug than trying to make VS2005 MSBuild do too much itself.

If you want, I can turn this into a full working VS2005 solution layout with:
`HandlerGui.csproj`, `Rust9xWindowsAuth.csproj`, and `BuildCopyTool.csproj` wired together.

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 17:58:00)*

The logging already tells you what's wrong:

```
RustProjectRoot: ..\..\..\..\rust-src
RustDllSourcePath: ..\..\..\..\rust-src\target\dll-debug\...
```

Those are **still relative paths**.

MSBuild properties don't automatically become absolute just because you concatenate them. If the working directory changes (and it often does during a build), `Exists()` can suddenly fail.

## First thing I'd do

Log the absolute path.

```xml
<PropertyGroup>
    <RustProjectRoot>$([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath('$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\..\..\..\..\rust-src'))</RustProjectRoot>
</PropertyGroup>
```

or

```xml
<PropertyGroup>
    <RustProjectRoot>$([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath('$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)..\..\..\..\rust-src'))
</RustProjectRoot>
```

Then rebuild.

The log should become

```
RustProjectRoot:
E:\code\rust9x-windows2000auth\rust-src

RustDllSourcePath:
E:\code\rust9x-windows2000auth\rust-src\target\dll-debug\...
```

instead of

```
..\..\..\..\rust-src
```

---

## Also log whether the file exists

MSBuild has no nice Exists() printout, so I usually do

```xml
<Exec
    Command='cmd /c if exist "$(RustDllSourcePath)" (echo FOUND) else (echo MISSING)' />
```

or even

```xml
<Exec
    Command='cmd /c dir "$(RustDllSourcePath)"'
    IgnoreExitCode="true"/>
```

which prints the actual Windows error.

---

## Better yet: let C# figure it out

For something like this I actually prefer a tiny helper executable over fighting MSBuild.

Example:

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;

class Program
{
    static int Main(string[] args)
    {
        string source = args[0];
        string dest = args[1];

        Console.WriteLine("Rust Copy");
        Console.WriteLine("Source      : " + source);
        Console.WriteLine("Destination : " + dest);

        Console.WriteLine("Full source : " + Path.GetFullPath(source));
        Console.WriteLine("Full dest   : " + Path.GetFullPath(dest));

        if (!File.Exists(source))
        {
            Console.WriteLine("SOURCE NOT FOUND");
            return 1;
        }

        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(dest));

        File.Copy(source, dest, true);

        Console.WriteLine("Copied successfully.");
        return 0;
    }
}
```

Then from MSBuild

```xml
<Exec Command="RustCopy.exe &quot;$(RustDllSourcePath)&quot; &quot;$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)&quot;" />
```

Now you get proper logging, exceptions, stack traces, and it's much easier to debug than MSBuild XML.

---

## I also suspect your relative path is simply wrong

Your project is here

```
E:\code\rust9x-windows2000auth\net-framework-gui\Brutus\Rust9xWindowsAuth\
```

and you're using

```
..\..\..\..\rust-src
```

Let's count:

```
Rust9xWindowsAuth
↑ ..
Brutus
↑ ..
net-framework-gui
↑ ..
rust9x-windows2000auth
↑ ..
E:\code
```

Four `..` components land you at:

```
E:\code\rust-src
```

not

```
E:\code\rust9x-windows2000auth\rust-src
```

If `rust-src` is inside the repository root, the correct path is almost certainly:

```
..\..\..\rust-src
```

Only **three** `..` components.

I would verify this first, because from the directory structure shown in your log, the current path appears to overshoot the repository root by one directory.

Below is a plain VS2005-friendly **BuildCopyTool** console app and its project file.

## `BuildCopyTool.csproj`

```xml
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <PropertyGroup>
    <Configuration Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == '' ">Debug</Configuration>
    <Platform Condition=" '$(Platform)' == '' ">AnyCPU</Platform>

    <ProductVersion>8.0.50727</ProductVersion>
    <SchemaVersion>2.0</SchemaVersion>
    <ProjectGuid>{D2C3C8B0-0D9A-4C2A-8D4F-5E4D4B4A4D21}</ProjectGuid>

    <OutputType>Exe</OutputType>
    <AppDesignerFolder>Properties</AppDesignerFolder>
    <RootNamespace>BuildCopyTool</RootNamespace>
    <AssemblyName>BuildCopyTool</AssemblyName>
    <TargetFrameworkVersion>v2.0</TargetFrameworkVersion>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Debug|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugSymbols>true</DebugSymbols>
    <DebugType>full</DebugType>
    <Optimize>false</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Debug\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>DEBUG;TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <AllowUnsafeBlocks>false</AllowUnsafeBlocks>
    <PlatformTarget>AnyCPU</PlatformTarget>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Release|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugType>pdbonly</DebugType>
    <Optimize>true</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Release\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <AllowUnsafeBlocks>false</AllowUnsafeBlocks>
    <PlatformTarget>AnyCPU</PlatformTarget>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Reference Include="System" />
    <Reference Include="System.Xml" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Compile Include="Program.cs" />
    <Compile Include="Properties\AssemblyInfo.cs" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <Import Project="$(MSBuildBinPath)\Microsoft.CSharp.targets" />
</Project>
```

## `Program.cs`

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;

namespace BuildCopyTool
{
    internal static class Program
    {
        private static int Main(string[] args)
        {
            try
            {
                Console.WriteLine("=== BuildCopyTool ===");

                if (args.Length < 3)
                {
                    PrintUsage();
                    return 2;
                }

                string sourceFile = args[0];
                string sourceRuntimeDir = args[1];
                string destinationDir = args[2];

                sourceFile = NormalizePath(sourceFile);
                sourceRuntimeDir = NormalizePath(sourceRuntimeDir);
                destinationDir = NormalizePath(destinationDir);

                Console.WriteLine("Source DLL      : " + sourceFile);
                Console.WriteLine("Source runtime  : " + sourceRuntimeDir);
                Console.WriteLine("Destination dir : " + destinationDir);
                Console.WriteLine();

                DumpDirectory("SOURCE DLL PARENT", Path.GetDirectoryName(sourceFile));
                DumpDirectory("SOURCE RUNTIME", sourceRuntimeDir);

                if (!File.Exists(sourceFile))
                {
                    Console.WriteLine("ERROR: DLL not found: " + sourceFile);
                    return 3;
                }

                if (!Directory.Exists(destinationDir))
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(destinationDir);
                    Console.WriteLine("Created destination directory.");
                }

                string dllDest = Path.Combine(destinationDir, Path.GetFileName(sourceFile));
                CopyFile(sourceFile, dllDest);

                CopyRuntimeFiles(sourceRuntimeDir, destinationDir);

                Console.WriteLine();
                Console.WriteLine("BuildCopyTool completed successfully.");
                return 0;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine();
                Console.WriteLine("FATAL ERROR:");
                Console.WriteLine(ex.ToString());
                return 1;
            }
        }

        private static void PrintUsage()
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Usage:");
            Console.WriteLine("  BuildCopyTool.exe <sourceDll> <sourceRuntimeDir> <destinationDir>");
            Console.WriteLine();
            Console.WriteLine("Example:");
            Console.WriteLine("  BuildCopyTool.exe");
            Console.WriteLine("    \"E:\\repo\\rust-src\\target\\dll-release\\i686-rust9x-windows-msvc\\release\\rust9x_windows_auth.dll\"");
            Console.WriteLine("    \"E:\\repo\\rust-src\\target\\dll-release\\i686-rust9x-windows-msvc\\release\"");
            Console.WriteLine("    \"E:\\repo\\net-framework-gui\\Brutus\\HandlerGui\\bin\\Debug\"");
        }

        private static string NormalizePath(string path)
        {
            if (path == null)
                return string.Empty;

            path = path.Trim().Trim('"');

            if (path.Length == 0)
                return string.Empty;

            try
            {
                return Path.GetFullPath(path);
            }
            catch
            {
                return path;
            }
        }

        private static void DumpDirectory(string label, string dir)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("=== " + label + " ===");

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir))
            {
                Console.WriteLine("(empty path)");
                return;
            }

            if (!Directory.Exists(dir))
            {
                Console.WriteLine("Missing: " + dir);
                return;
            }

            Console.WriteLine("Path: " + dir);

            string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(dir);
            Console.WriteLine("Files: " + files.Length);
            for (int i = 0; i < files.Length; i++)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("  " + Path.GetFileName(files[i]));
            }

            string crtDir = Path.Combine(dir, "Microsoft.VC80.CRT");
            if (Directory.Exists(crtDir))
            {
                string[] crtFiles = Directory.GetFiles(crtDir);
                Console.WriteLine("CRT files: " + crtFiles.Length);
                for (int j = 0; j < crtFiles.Length; j++)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine("  Microsoft.VC80.CRT\\" + Path.GetFileName(crtFiles[j]));
                }
            }

            Console.WriteLine();
        }

        private static void CopyRuntimeFiles(string sourceRuntimeDir, string destinationDir)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("=== CopyRuntimeFiles ===");

            if (!Directory.Exists(sourceRuntimeDir))
            {
                Console.WriteLine("Runtime directory missing: " + sourceRuntimeDir);
                return;
            }

            string[] rootFiles = Directory.GetFiles(sourceRuntimeDir);
            for (int i = 0; i < rootFiles.Length; i++)
            {
                string file = rootFiles[i];
                string name = Path.GetFileName(file);

                string lower = name.ToLowerInvariant();
                if (lower.EndsWith(".pdb"))
                    continue;

                if (string.Compare(name, Path.GetFileName(destinationDir), true) == 0)
                    continue;

                string dest = Path.Combine(destinationDir, name);
                CopyFile(file, dest);
            }

            string crtSource = Path.Combine(sourceRuntimeDir, "Microsoft.VC80.CRT");
            if (Directory.Exists(crtSource))
            {
                string crtDest = Path.Combine(destinationDir, "Microsoft.VC80.CRT");
                if (!Directory.Exists(crtDest))
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(crtDest);

                string[] crtFiles = Directory.GetFiles(crtSource);
                for (int j = 0; j < crtFiles.Length; j++)
                {
                    string crtFile = crtFiles[j];
                    string crtDestFile = Path.Combine(crtDest, Path.GetFileName(crtFile));
                    CopyFile(crtFile, crtDestFile);
                }
            }

            Console.WriteLine("Runtime copy complete.");
        }

        private static void CopyFile(string source, string destination)
        {
            string destDir = Path.GetDirectoryName(destination);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(destDir) && !Directory.Exists(destDir))
                Directory.CreateDirectory(destDir);

            Console.WriteLine("Copy: " + source);
            Console.WriteLine("  -> " + destination);

            File.Copy(source, destination, true);
        }
    }
}
```

## How to call it from your main project

Use an `Exec` like this in the C# app `.csproj`:

```xml
<Target Name="AfterBuild">
  <Exec Command="&quot;$(SolutionDir)BuildCopyTool\bin\$(Configuration)\BuildCopyTool.exe&quot; &quot;$(RustDllSourcePath)&quot; &quot;$(RustRuntimeDir)&quot; &quot;$(TargetDir)&quot;" />
</Target>
```

If you want the Rust project to build the tool first, add a project reference or build order in the solution, then call it after the Rust DLL is produced.

  <Target Name="BeforeBuild">
    <Message Text="=== Rust9xWindowsAuth BeforeBuild ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Configuration: $(Configuration)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Platform: $(Platform)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="ProjectDir: $(ProjectDir)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustProjectRoot: $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustDllSourcePath: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustRuntimeDir: $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />

    <Exec Command="cmd /c if exist &quot;$(RustRuntimeDir)&quot; dir /s /b &quot;$(RustRuntimeDir)&quot; > &quot;$(ProjectDir)rust-build-inputs.txt&quot; else echo RustRuntimeDir missing > &quot;$(ProjectDir)rust-build-inputs.txt&quot;"
          ContinueOnError="true" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="BuildRustProject" Condition="'$(BuildRust)' == 'true'">
    <Message Text="Building Rust project in $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-debug" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Debug' " />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-release" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Release' " />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="AfterBuild">
    <CallTarget Targets="CopyRustDll" />
    <CallTarget Targets="CopyRustRuntimeFiles" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="CopyRustDll">
    <Message Text="=== CopyRustDll ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Source: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Destination: $(TargetDir)" Importance="high" />

    <Error Text="Rust DLL not found: $(RustDllSourcePath)"
           Condition="!Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="$(RustDllSourcePath)"
          DestinationFolder="$(TargetDir)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false"
          OverwriteReadOnlyFiles="true" />

    <Exec Command="cmd /c dir /b &quot;$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)&quot;"
          ContinueOnError="true" />

    <Message Text="Rust DLL copy completed." Importance="high" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="CopyRustRuntimeFiles">
    <Message Text="=== CopyRustRuntimeFiles ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Runtime dir: $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />

    <ItemGroup Condition="Exists('$(RustRuntimeDir)')">
      <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.dll" Exclude="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.pdb" />
      <RustRuntimeRootFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\*.manifest" />
      <RustRuntimeCrtFiles Include="$(RustRuntimeDir)\Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" />
    </ItemGroup>

    <Message Text="Root runtime files count: @(RustRuntimeRootFiles->Count())" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="CRT runtime files count: @(RustRuntimeCrtFiles->Count())" Importance="high" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(TargetDir)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false"
          OverwriteReadOnlyFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeRootFiles)' != ''" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false"
          OverwriteReadOnlyFiles="true"
          Condition="'@(RustRuntimeCrtFiles)' != ''" />

    <Exec Command="cmd /c dir /s /b &quot;$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT&quot;"
          ContinueOnError="true" />

    <Message Text="Rust runtime copy completed." Importance="high" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="Clean">
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)$(AssemblyName).dll" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)$(AssemblyName).dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcr80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcr80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcp80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcp80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcm80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcm80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <RemoveDir Directories="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <Message Text="Rust artifacts cleaned." Importance="high" />
  </Target>
</Project>

**ChatGPT** *(05.08.2026, 18:02:39)*

Use `BuildCopyTool` as the only copy mechanism here, and let MSBuild just log and call it.

Replace your `Rust9xWindowsAuth.csproj` with this:

```xml
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <PropertyGroup>
    <Configuration Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == '' ">Debug</Configuration>
    <Platform Condition=" '$(Platform)' == '' ">AnyCPU</Platform>

    <ProductVersion>8.0.50727</ProductVersion>
    <SchemaVersion>2.0</SchemaVersion>
    <ProjectGuid>{86E365E2-D855-4579-B53D-9B0DF7FF0857}</ProjectGuid>

    <OutputType>Library</OutputType>
    <AppDesignerFolder>Properties</AppDesignerFolder>
    <RootNamespace>Rust9xWindowsAuth</RootNamespace>
    <AssemblyName>Rust9xWindowsAuth</AssemblyName>
    <TargetFrameworkVersion>v2.0</TargetFrameworkVersion>

    <!-- Rust build source -->
    <RustProjectRoot>$(ProjectDir)..\..\..\rust-src</RustProjectRoot>
    <RustDllName>rust9x_windows_auth.dll</RustDllName>
    <RustTargetTriple>i686-rust9x-windows-msvc</RustTargetTriple>

    <RustDllDebugPath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug\$(RustDllName)</RustDllDebugPath>
    <RustDllReleasePath>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release\$(RustDllName)</RustDllReleasePath>

    <RustRuntimeDebugDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-debug\$(RustTargetTriple)\debug</RustRuntimeDebugDir>
    <RustRuntimeReleaseDir>$(RustProjectRoot)\target\dll-release\$(RustTargetTriple)\release</RustRuntimeReleaseDir>

    <!-- BuildCopyTool location -->
    <BuildCopyToolExe Condition="'$(BuildCopyToolExe)'=='' and '$(SolutionDir)'!=''">$(SolutionDir)BuildCopyTool\bin\$(Configuration)\BuildCopyTool.exe</BuildCopyToolExe>
    <BuildCopyToolExe Condition="'$(BuildCopyToolExe)'==''">$(ProjectDir)..\BuildCopyTool\bin\$(Configuration)\BuildCopyTool.exe</BuildCopyToolExe>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Debug|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugSymbols>true</DebugSymbols>
    <DebugType>full</DebugType>
    <Optimize>false</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Debug\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>DEBUG;TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <AllowUnsafeBlocks>true</AllowUnsafeBlocks>
    <PlatformTarget>AnyCPU</PlatformTarget>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllDebugPath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeDebugDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <PropertyGroup Condition=" '$(Configuration)|$(Platform)' == 'Release|AnyCPU' ">
    <DebugType>pdbonly</DebugType>
    <Optimize>true</Optimize>
    <OutputPath>bin\Release\</OutputPath>
    <DefineConstants>TRACE</DefineConstants>
    <ErrorReport>prompt</ErrorReport>
    <WarningLevel>4</WarningLevel>
    <RustDllSourcePath>$(RustDllReleasePath)</RustDllSourcePath>
    <RustRuntimeDir>$(RustRuntimeReleaseDir)</RustRuntimeDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Reference Include="System" />
    <Reference Include="System.Configuration" />
    <Reference Include="System.Data" />
    <Reference Include="System.Xml" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <Compile Include="AuthConfig.cs" />
    <Compile Include="AuthManager.cs" />
    <Compile Include="CentralFile.cs" />
    <Compile Include="Properties\AssemblyInfo.cs" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <None Include="App.config" />
  </ItemGroup>

  <Import Project="$(MSBuildBinPath)\Microsoft.CSharp.targets" />

  <!-- Old-style VS2005 chaining -->
  <PropertyGroup>
    <BeforeBuildDependsOn>$(BeforeBuildDependsOn);LogRustPaths</BeforeBuildDependsOn>
    <BuildDependsOn>$(BuildDependsOn);BuildRustProject</BuildDependsOn>
    <AfterBuildDependsOn>$(AfterBuildDependsOn);RunBuildCopyTool</AfterBuildDependsOn>
    <CleanDependsOn>$(CleanDependsOn);CleanRustFiles</CleanDependsOn>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <Target Name="LogRustPaths">
    <Message Text="=== Rust9xWindowsAuth LogRustPaths ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Configuration: $(Configuration)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Platform: $(Platform)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="ProjectDir: $(ProjectDir)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustProjectRoot: $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustDllSourcePath: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RustRuntimeDir: $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="BuildCopyToolExe: $(BuildCopyToolExe)" Importance="high" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="BuildRustProject" Condition="'$(BuildRust)' == 'true'">
    <Message Text="Building Rust project in $(RustProjectRoot)" Importance="high" />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-debug" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Debug' " />
    <Exec Command="cargo +rust9x dll-release" WorkingDirectory="$(RustProjectRoot)" Condition=" '$(Configuration)' == 'Release' " />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="RunBuildCopyTool">
    <Message Text="=== RunBuildCopyTool ===" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="Tool: $(BuildCopyToolExe)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="DLL : $(RustDllSourcePath)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="RT  : $(RustRuntimeDir)" Importance="high" />
    <Message Text="OUT : $(TargetDir)" Importance="high" />

    <Error Text="BuildCopyTool not found: $(BuildCopyToolExe)" Condition="!Exists('$(BuildCopyToolExe)')" />
    <Error Text="Rust DLL not found: $(RustDllSourcePath)" Condition="!Exists('$(RustDllSourcePath)')" />

    <Exec Command="&quot;$(BuildCopyToolExe)&quot; &quot;$(RustDllSourcePath)&quot; &quot;$(RustRuntimeDir)&quot; &quot;$(TargetDir)&quot;" />
  </Target>

  <Target Name="CleanRustFiles">
    <Delete Files="$(OutputPath)$(AssemblyName).dll" Condition="Exists('$(OutputPath)$(AssemblyName).dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(RustDllName)')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcr80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcr80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcp80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcp80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)msvcm80.dll" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)msvcm80.dll')" />
    <Delete Files="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT\*.*" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <RemoveDir Directories="$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)Microsoft.VC80.CRT')" />
    <Message Text="Rust artifacts cleaned." Importance="high" />
  </Target>
</Project>
```

The important changes are that the build now logs the resolved paths, fails hard if the native DLL or copy tool is missing, and calls the copy tool after the managed assembly is built.

The next place to patch is `HandlerGui.csproj` the same way, but there the `BuildCopyTool` call should copy the native DLL into `HandlerGui\bin\...` as well, because the managed project reference will not reliably bring the native Rust DLL along.
